TL;DR: 本文提出Liouville路径传输的几何框架，将非线性光谱中的谱线畸变、峰位偏移等特征解释为Liouville空间流形曲率导致的几何签名，而非唯象展宽修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将Liouville路径视为独立演化的固定对象，忽略了开放量子系统中环境相互作用导致的路径间振幅连续再分布（传输）效应，现有框架无法解释由此产生的可观测光谱特征。

Method: 构建以Liouvillian联络为核心的几何框架，引入Liouvillian曲率和观测和乐（observational holonomy）；利用Duhamel展开Liouvillian传播子，推导出从观测数据中重构路径传输算子的重构程序。

Result: 证明了基矢不匹配（环境指针基与观测基）是路径传输的根源；导出曲率与谱畸变的定量关系；实现了在全基矢失准范围内传输算子的可重构性。

Conclusion: 光谱特征不仅取决于路径的存在性，更取决于振幅如何在路径间传输；路径几何结构是非线性光谱中一个互补的、被忽视的组织层次。

Abstract: Liouville pathways provide the conceptual foundation for interpreting multidimensional spectroscopies, yet are typically treated as fixed objects that evolve independently between optical interactions. In open quantum systems, this picture is incomplete. Environmental interactions continuously redistribute amplitude among pathways during every free-evolution interval, generating transport that leaves measurable signatures in the nonlinear spectroscopic response. We develop a geometric framework in which pathway transport is governed by a Liouvillian connection, its associated curvature, and the resulting observational holonomy. The framework applies to open quantum systems in which the environment selects a pointer basis distinct from the observational basis used to construct the spectroscopic response. This basis incompatibility induces transport among Liouville pathways, generating characteristic spectral distortions and a nontrivial Liouvillian curvature. Using a Duhamel expansion of the Liouvillian propagator, we derive a reconstruction procedure that identifies the transport operators responsible for the observed redistribution of pathway weight, accurate throughout the full range of basis misalignment. This perspective reframes spectral features as determined not only by which pathways exist but by how amplitude is transported among them. Spectral distortions, peak shifts, and otherwise-forbidden pathway contributions become geometric signatures of a curved Liouville-space manifold rather than phenomenological broadening corrections, identifying pathway geometry as a complementary layer of organization in nonlinear spectroscopy.

</details>


### [91] [The Quantum Hamming Bound in Arbitrary Local Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22538)
*Yu-Xuan Zhang,Jing-Ling Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对于任意局域维度，证明了有限长度量子Hamming界成立，表明简并性虽然能使错误扇区合并，但不足以打破Hamming计数的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是简并性（不同物理错误在码子空间上重合）导致的球堆积过计数是否可能使Hamming不等式失效。此前线性规划、渐近和结构结果尚未解决任意精确子空间码的逐点有限长度问题。

Method: 将任意可能的违反约化为双中心Hamming球交集不等式（由Lloyd响应归一化）。对q≥4，利用Lloyd平方线性规划在约化到字母表Q≥16且临界长度n=4t+1后的均匀半间隙。对qutrits（q=3），通过二次滤波Lloyd平方、精确系数证书约简和Stein切线正性论证处理边界情况。

Result: 对每个q≥3证明了非二进制范围的量子Hamming界，二进制端点由独立的Q=4计费几何处理。

Conclusion: 简并性可能合并错误扇区，但不足以击败Hamming计数，从而证明了任意局域维度下的有限长度量子Hamming界。

Abstract: The quantum Hamming bound is the finite-length sphere-packing count for exact quantum error correction: the code dimension times the number of correctable local error patterns must fit inside the ambient Hilbert space. For nondegenerate codes this follows from disjoint error spheres. Degeneracy is the only obstruction, because distinct physical errors can coincide on the code subspace and turn sphere packing into an overcount. The central finite-length question has been whether this overcount can ever invalidate the Hamming inequality. Earlier linear-programming, asymptotic, and structural results left a pointwise finite-length problem for arbitrary exact subspace codes. Writing $Q=q^2$ for the Hamming-scheme alphabet, the nonbinary range begins at $Q=9$; here we prove the bound for every $q\ge3$, while the binary endpoint is governed by a distinct $Q=4$ charging geometry. For every nontrivial exact subspace code in this range, any possible violation reduces to a two-center Hamming-ball intersection inequality normalized by the Lloyd response. For $q\ge4$, the Lloyd-square linear program has a uniform half-gap after reduction to alphabet $Q\ge16$ and critical length $n=4t+1$. Qutrits form the boundary: the half-gap disappears, but the bridge is handled by a quadratic-filtered Lloyd square, an exact coefficient-certificate reduction, and a Stein-tangent positivity argument. Thus degeneracy may merge error sectors, but not enough to beat the Hamming count. This proves the nonbinary part of the finite-length quantum Hamming bound; together with the independent binary endpoint theorem, it gives the result in arbitrary local dimension.

</details>


### [92] [Hierarchical Logical Processor on the Rotated Surface Code with Shuttle Buses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22594)
*Zi-Han Chen,Ming-Cheng Chen,Chao-Yang Lu,Jian-Wei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出层级逻辑处理器（HLP），通过将高码率量子CSS码与旋转表面码（RSC）级联，在保持超越RSC编码效率的同时，将长程连接需求降至每Θ(d₀)轮纠错一次。HLP引入 shuttle bus 概念，利用横向混合单元CNOT门实现高效的层级综合征提取与高度并行的逻辑Pauli测量。在10⁻³物理错误率下，基于[[256,194,4]]码的HLP相比标准RSC实现3-4倍量子比特效率提升，相比同层级 yoke surface code 每逻辑量子比特节省100-200物理量子比特，逻辑纠错周期缩短20-30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超平面连接量子平台为容错量子计算提供新机遇，但qLDPC码的直接实现需要每轮综合征提取中引入非局域耦合，带来额外物理错误与实现复杂度。为降低此类耦合的频率，提出HLP架构。

Method: 将高码率量子CSS码与旋转表面码级联，形成层级结构；引入elongated RSC patches（shuttle buses），利用transversal hybrid-unit CNOT gates使单个shuttle bus同时耦合多个标准RSC patches，实现高效的level-1综合征提取并抑制误差关联，支持高度并行的逻辑Pauli测量。

Result: 在10⁻³物理错误率下，基于[[256,194,4]]码的HLP相比标准RSC实现3-4倍量子比特效率提升；相比同层级yoked surface code，每逻辑量子比特节省100-200物理量子比特，逻辑纠错周期缩短20-30倍。

Conclusion: HLP通过层级级联大幅降低长程连接需求频率，在保持高编码效率的同时显著提升容错量子计算的实用性与可实现性。

Abstract: Quantum platforms with beyond-planar connectivity provide new opportunities for fault-tolerant quantum computation (FTQC). While quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes offer high encoding efficiency, their direct implementation requires non-local couplings in every round of syndrome extraction, incurring additional physical error and implementation complexity. To reduce the frequency of such couplings, we propose the Hierarchical Logical Processor (HLP), which concatenates a high-rate quantum CSS code with the rotated surface code (RSC). HLPs can achieve beyond-RSC encoding efficiency while requiring long-range connectivity only once every $Θ(d_0)$ rounds of level-0 error correction, where $d_0$ denotes the base-code distance, substantially reducing the frequency of non-local couplings relative to direct implementations of qLDPC codes. HLPs introduce elongated RSC patches called shuttle buses. Using transversal hybrid-unit CNOT gates, a single shuttle bus can simultaneously couple to multiple standard RSC patches. This capability enables efficient level-1 syndrome extraction with suppressed level-1 error correlations and supports highly parallel logical Pauli measurements. We perform circuit-level simulations of several concrete HLP constructions and benchmark both logical memory and logical Pauli measurement performance. At a physical error rate of $10^{-3}$, an HLP based on the [[256,194,4]] code achieves 3-4 times higher qubit efficiency than the standard RSC. Compared with the yoked surface code on the same level-1 code, this HLP reduces the space overhead per logical qubit by 100-200 physical qubits and shortens the logical error-correction cycle time by a factor of 20-30.

</details>


### [93] [Generalization of catability for parity-less cat states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22641)
*Michal Matulík,Jan Provazník,Petr Marek,Šimon Bräuer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将基于零化子的可猫性认证框架从奇偶定义的相干猫态扩展到无奇偶性的Kerr猫态，通过引入位移-奇偶算符替代原有奇偶分量，实现了无需完整态层析的可观测认证方法，数值验证表明该方法对光学损耗具有更强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展可猫性认证框架以适用于无奇偶性的Kerr猫态，解决原有奇偶零化子无法区分Kerr猫态的问题，提供实验上可行的表征方法。

Method: 引入位移-奇偶算符替代原零化子中的奇偶分量，捕获Kerr猫态的特征干涉结构；通过有限数量的光子数测量直接评估广义可猫性，无需完整态层析；同时识别出基于复共轭的精确反线性零化子作为理想代数描述。

Result: 数值基准测试表明该方法能忠实认证Kerr猫特征，产生理想Kerr猫态的精确近似，且对光学损耗的鲁棒性优于基于保真度的认证方法。

Conclusion: 拓宽了可猫性框架的适用范围，为Kerr猫态的表征提供了实验上实用的方案。

Abstract: We extend the nullifier-based certification framework of catability from parity-defined coherent cat states to parity-less Kerr cat states. Since Kerr cats cannot be distinguished by parity alone, we replace the parity component of the original nullifier with a displaced-parity operator that captures their characteristic interference structure. The resulting generalized catability remains directly observable and can be evaluated from a finite number of photon-number measurements without full state tomography. Numerical benchmarks show that the method faithfully certifies Kerr-cat features, produces accurate approximations of ideal Kerr cat states, and is more resilient to optical loss than fidelity-based certification. We also identify an exact anti-linear nullifier based on complex conjugation, providing an ideal algebraic description of Kerr cat states. Our results broaden the scope of catability and provide an experimentally practical approach to the characterization of Kerr cat states.

</details>


### [94] [A Measurement-Like Test of Continuous Spontaneous Localization with a Reversible Nanoparticle Pointer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22707)
*Peter Renkel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于测量类干涉的连续自发定位（CSL）测试方案，利用悬浮纳米粒子作为可逆介观指针，通过分支条件性位移放大路径信息，在普通量子力学下可恢复微观相干性，而CSL则导致不可逆的可见度损失。对于10⁻¹⁸ kg的纳米粒子，在10⁻²¹ N差分力、0.4 K温度条件下，可获得λ_min≈1.4×10⁻¹⁰ s⁻¹（r_c=100 nm, 10⁵次测量）和λ_min≈8.7×10⁻¹² s⁻¹的灵敏度，相比直接物质波CSL界限提升约7×10³倍和10⁵倍，旨在实现更强的直接可见度损失CSL测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CSL模型的直接干涉测试灵敏度有限，需要发展新型实验方案以在更高灵敏度下直接检验CSL导致的量子相干性损失，同时探索可逆测量类实现坍缩敏感性的新途径。

Method: 采用测量类干涉结构：微观二分枝系统对悬浮纳米粒子施加相反的弱力，产生分支条件性指针位移（放大路径信息），经一个弱阱周期后指针位置和动量重新组合。在普通量子力学下恢复微观相干性，而CSL导致指针质量分布分离期间的不可逆可见度损失。

Result: 对10⁻¹⁸ kg纳米粒子、10⁻²¹ N差分力、0.4 K温度条件，在r_c=100 nm、10⁵次测量下获得λ_min≈1.4×10⁻¹⁰ s⁻¹；更优低频点达λ_min≈8.7×10⁻¹² s⁻¹。灵敏度分别比直接物质波CSL界限提升约7×10³倍和10⁵倍。

Conclusion: 该方案旨在实现比现有直接可见度损失测试更强的CSL检验，同时实现可逆测量类坍缩敏感性的实验验证，但并不追求最强的整体排除曲线。

Abstract: We propose a measurement-like interferometric test of Continuous Spontaneous Localization (CSL), in which a trapped nanoparticle acts as a reversible mesoscopic pointer rather than as the initially prepared microscopic superposition. A microscopic two-branch system applies opposite weak forces to the nanoparticle, temporarily amplifying which-branch information into branch-conditioned pointer displacements. After one weak-trap period the pointer positions and momenta recombine, so ordinary quantum mechanics predicts recovery of the microscopic coherence, whereas CSL predicts an irreversible visibility loss accumulated while the pointer mass distributions were separated.
  For a $10^{-18},{\rm kg}$ nanoparticle driven by a $10^{-21},{\rm N}$ differential force at $T=0.4,{\rm K}$, including thermal breathing and an ordinary loss budget $Λ_{\rm loss}\lesssim 0.1$, we find $λ_{\min}\simeq 1.4\times 10^{-10},{\rm s}^{-1}$ at $r_c=100,{\rm nm}$ with $10^5$ shots. A more aggressive lower-frequency point reaches $λ_{\min}\simeq 8.7\times 10^{-12},{\rm s}^{-1}$.
  These sensitivities would improve over digitized direct matter-wave CSL bounds by about $7\times 10^3$ and $10^5$, respectively, while remaining above the strongest non-interferometric bounds. The proposal is therefore aimed at a substantially stronger direct visibility-loss test of CSL, and at a reversible measurement-like realization of collapse sensitivity, rather than at the strongest overall exclusion curve.

</details>


### [95] [Lewis-Ermakov approach for the time-dependent two-level system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22709)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为时间相关二能级系统构建显式的Lewis-Ermakov型动力学不变量，以解决其演化算子和传播子的精确求解问题，并扩展至非厄米耗散等复杂场景。

Method: 利用$su(2)$和$su(1,1)$代数（共同复化为$sl(2,\mathbb{C})$）与时间相关谐振子之间的代数对应关系，构造动力学不变量，进而获得闭合形式的演化算子和精确传播子；在辅助标度函数为实数时，通过逆向工程实现绝热捷径。

Result: 得到了适用于任意时间相关耦合$a(t)$和失谐$b(t)$的闭合形式演化算子和精确传播子，并在Landau-Zener跃迁、非厄米耗散过程和绝热快速通道等场景中验证了该方法；当辅助标度函数为实数时，实现了完全显式控制场的绝热捷径。

Conclusion: 该工作为时间相关二能级系统提供了一种统一的代数框架，能够高效处理多种物理场景，并通过逆向工程实现绝热捷径，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: We construct an explicit Lewis-Ermakov-type dynamical invariant for time-dependent two-level systems by exploiting their algebraic correspondence with the time-dependent harmonic oscillator through the $su(2)$ and $su(1,1)$ algebras, which share the common complexification $sl(2,\mathbb{C})$. This invariant provides a closed-form evolution operator and an exact propagator for arbitrary time-dependent coupling $a(t)$ and detuning $b(t)$. We illustrate the method on representative scenarios, including Landau-Zener transitions, non-Hermitian dissipative processes, and adiabatic rapid passage, and we obtain inverse-engineered shortcuts to adiabaticity with fully explicit control fields when the auxiliary scaling function is taken to be real.

</details>


### [96] [A single-electron double quantum dot with Rashba spin-orbit interaction as a working substance for heat machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22733)
*Ivan Romualdo de Oliveira,Vinícius N. A. Lula-Rocha,Moises Rojas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了以双量子点中单电子为工质、受磁场和Rashba自旋轨道耦合作用的量子奥托热机的热力学性能，发现Rashba相互作用可作为切换热机、制冷机、加热器和加速器四种工作模式的有效控制参数，并揭示了效率与提取功之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Rashba自旋轨道耦合如何影响量子热机的热力学性能，以及能否通过调控该相互作用实现不同工作模式（热机、制冷机等）的切换。

Method: 采用准静态奥托循环，通过分析Zeeman分裂、量子点间隧穿振幅和Rashba耦合控制的哈密顿量，计算与热库交换的热量、提取功和效率，并进行全局数值优化。

Result: Rashba相互作用可实现热机、制冷机、加热器和加速器四种模式的切换；最高效率接近Carnot界但出现在最大温度梯度附近，最大功输出则位于参数空间的不同区域，二者存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: Rashba耦合可作为量子热机的有效控制参数，Zeeman分裂、隧穿振幅和Rashba耦合引起的能谱形变共同决定了效率与功输出之间的最优权衡。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic performance of a quantum Otto machine whose working substance is a single electron confined in a double quantum dot under an external magnetic field and Rashba spin-orbit interaction. The Hamiltonian is controlled by the Zeeman splitting, the interdot tunneling amplitude, and the Rashba coupling, which induces spin-flip tunneling between localized orbital states. Within a quasistatic Otto cycle, we analyze the heat exchanged with the reservoirs, the extracted work, and the efficiency as functions of the Hamiltonian parameters and reservoir temperatures. We show that the Rashba interaction acts as an effective control parameter for switching among heat-engine, refrigerator, heater, and accelerator regimes. A global numerical analysis over the Hamiltonian parameters and reservoir temperatures identifies the optimal operating points for efficiency and work output in the heat-engine regime. The highest efficiencies occur near the maximum temperature gradient explored and approach the Carnot bound, whereas the largest work output appears in a different region of parameter space. The results reveal a clear trade-off between maximum efficiency and maximum extracted work, governed by the spectral deformation induced by the Zeeman splitting, tunneling amplitude, and Rashba coupling.

</details>


### [97] [All-optical Implementation of Generalized Quantum Teleportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22736)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测量基连续变量光量子计算受限于经典电子前馈电路的处理延迟和吞吐量瓶颈，亟需克服这一速度与带宽限制。

Method: 提出一种容错的全光前馈（AOFF）架构，用于广义量子隐形传态，可执行任意线性操作，并基于实际器件参数进行定量噪声分析。

Result: 定量噪声分析表明该架构成功抑制了硬件诱导的噪声基底，验证了其与容错量子计算要求的兼容性；通过消除光电转换，实现了连续高通量操作，显著缩短电路运行时间。

Abstract: Measurement-based continuous-variable optical quantum computing inherently offers high-speed, large-scale operations, yet its practical performance remains constrained by the processing latencies and throughput bottlenecks imposed by classical electronic feedforward circuits. To overcome these limitations, we propose a loss-tolerant, all-optical feedforward (AOFF) architecture for generalized quantum teleportation capable of executing arbitrary linear operations. Quantitative noise analysis under realistic device parameters demonstrates that the architecture successfully suppresses hardware-induced noise floor, confirming its compatibility with fault-tolerant quantum computing requirements. By eliminating optoelectronic conversions, this scheme enables continuous high-throughput operations that drastically reduce circuit runtime. Ultimately, this approach delivers a noise-resilient platform that reconciles operational versatility with the intrinsic speed and bandwidth of optical quantum information processing.

</details>

Motivation: 在混合量子系统中生成非经典磁振子态（如Wigner负性态），利用非相干泵浦作为可控耗散资源替代传统相干驱动方案。

Method: 结合量子比特非相干泵浦与色散磁振子数选择性，通过级联耗散机制将稳态布居定向至特定磁振子Fock态；推导解析生灭模型进行理论描述。

Abstract: We show that incoherently pumped qubits can realize a cascaded dissipative mechanism for stabilizing Wigner-negative magnon steady states. The mechanism combines qubit pumping with dispersive magnon-number selectivity to direct the steady-state population toward selected magnon Fock states. In the single-qubit case, the single-magnon population can approach unity, accompanied by strong antibunching and pronounced Wigner negativity. Extending the same principle to multiple qubits yields Wigner-negative steady states dominated by higher magnon Fock components. We further derive an analytical birth--death model that captures the mechanism and agrees with numerical results. These results establish incoherent qubit pumping as a controllable dissipative resource for generating nonclassical magnon states in hybrid quantum systems.

</details>


### [99] [Linear optical Bell state measurement for rotation-symmetric cat codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22832)
*Issa Oe,Suguru Endo,Rui Asaoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对旋转对称猫（RS-cat）码提出了一种仅使用半束分束器（HBS）和光子数分辨探测器（PNRD）的贝尔态测量（BSM）协议，利用RS-cat码的离散旋转对称性诱导的光子数结构来提取模光子数与相位信息。在理想无损耗条件下，该BSM协议对任意对称阶数N和大振幅α具有确定性；在光子损耗下，高阶RS-cat码在特定损耗区域具有优势，且后选择可提升成功概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高效贝尔态测量（BSM）是长距离量子通信和融合型量子计算（FBQC）的关键原始操作，而RS-cat码作为一种兼具有限能量可实现性和抗光子损耗鲁棒性的玻色子编码平台，需要开发高效的BSM协议以发挥其优势。

Method: 利用RS-cat码离散旋转对称性诱导的特征性光子数结构，设计仅使用半束分束器（HBS）和光子数分辨探测器（PNRD）的BSM协议，通过同时提取光子数模信息和相位信息实现贝尔态判别。

Result: (1) 理想无损耗条件下，对任意对称阶数N和足够大振幅α，协议具有确定性；(2) 数值评估了光子损耗下的成功概率，识别出高阶RS-cat码具有优势的损耗区域；(3) 后选择可进一步提升成功概率。

Conclusion: 提出的HBS+PNRD的BSM协议为RS-cat码在长距离量子通信和FBQC中的应用提供了可行且高效的贝尔态测量方案，高阶RS-cat码在抗损耗方面具有潜在优势。

Abstract: Rotation-symmetric cat (RS-cat) codes are a bosonic-code platform for quantum information processing, combining finite-energy realizability with robustness against photon loss through their discrete rotational symmetry. For applications in long-distance quantum communication and fusion-based quantum computation (FBQC), efficient Bell state measurement (BSM) is a key primitive. In this work, we consider a BSM protocol for RS-cat codes using only a half beam splitter (HBS) and photon-number-resolving detectors (PNRDs). By exploiting the characteristic photon-number structure induced by the discrete rotational symmetry of RS-cat codes, our protocol extracts both photon-number modulo and phase information for Bell-state discrimination. We show that, under ideal loss-free conditions, the proposed BSM protocol becomes deterministic for arbitrary symmetry order $N$ for sufficiently large amplitudes $α$. We further numerically evaluate the success probability under photon loss and identify the loss regime in which higher-order RS-cat codes provide an advantage. Finally, we show that post-selection can enhance the success probability.

</details>


### [100] [When the Learning With Errors Problem Meets the Coherent Ising Machine: A Penalty-Free Algorithm-Hardware Co-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22843)
*Shuxian Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CIM-BDD，一种将LWE问题通过无惩罚映射转化为QUBO问题的混合有界距离解码求解器，并在Coherent Ising Machine上成功验证了40维LWE实例的端到端破解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LWE问题是后量子密码学的数学基础，对其进行密码分析（包括经典格归约、机器学习及量子-经典混合方法）具有重要意义。现有方法在NISQ设备上存在惩罚项引入的近似误差、量子比特需求过高等局限。

Method: (1) 通过代数消元将LWE嵌入q-ary格，转化为最近向量问题(CVP)；(2) 将平方误差范数直接作为QUBO能量（无惩罚映射）；(3) 设计CR-BNP投影驱动的自适应混合基编码，减少量子比特数和QUBO系数范围；(4) 推导统计有界早停阈值T_early，兼作Decision-LWE区分器。

Result: 在TU Darmstadt LWE Challenge上，于Coherent Ising Machine CPQC-550上实现了40维Search-LWE和Decision-LWE实例的端到端演示验证。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子-经典混合密码分析的新算法-硬件协同设计范式，为NISQ设备上的LWE密码分析提供了可行的实现路径。

Abstract: The Learning With Errors (LWE) problem constitutes the mathematical foundation of modern Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Cryptanalysis of LWE ranges from classical lattice reduction to machine learning and quantum-classical hybrids. We propose CIM-BDD, a hybrid Bounded-Distance-Decoding solver that reduces LWE to a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) problem through a strictly \emph{penalty-free} mapping. An algebraic elimination of the secret embeds LWE into a $q$-ary lattice, absorbing the modular arithmetic and recasting the problem as a Closest Vector Problem (CVP). The squared error norm is then used \emph{directly} as the QUBO energy, so the cryptographic noise is the objective to be minimized rather than a penalized constraint. To realize this general model on current Noisy Intermediate-Scale Quantum (NISQ) devices, we design a special encoding method: a Continuous Relaxed Babai's Nearest Plane (CR-BNP) projection drives an adaptive mixed-radix encoder that greatly reduces both the qubit count and the QUBO coefficient range, so that a single batched hardware submission suffices. We further derive a statistically bounded early-stopping threshold ($T_{\text{early}}$) that acts as a one-sided certificate and doubles as a Decision-LWE distinguisher. We validate the framework on the TU Darmstadt LWE Challenge, giving an end-to-end demonstration for both Search- and Decision-LWE of a $40$-dimensional instance on the Coherent Ising Machine CPQC-550. This work establishes a new algorithm-hardware co-design paradigm for quantum-classical hybrid cryptanalysis.

</details>


### [101] [Bunny Codes: Broadening Superconducting Quantum Error Correction Capability through Advanced Control Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22853)
*Runshi Zhou,Xingye Yuan,Linghang Kong,Fang Zhang,Kai Zhang,Zhaohui Yang,Jianxin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用超导量子比特扩展门集（CNOT和CXSWAP）来简化具有非局部稳定子的qLDPC码的症候提取，发现了在二维最近邻量子比特连接架构上性能优于toric码和surface码的"Bunny codes"，在周期边界条件下码率约为toric码的3倍，开放边界条件下约为旋转surface码的2倍，且在电路级仿真中逻辑错误率比toric码低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用超导量子比特硬件层面已解锁的扩展原生门集（而非物理层面实现长程耦合器）来简化qLDPC码的症候提取，从而降低硬件实现复杂度。

Method: 利用超导量子比特的扩展原生双量子比特门集（CNOT和CXSWAP），通过穷举搜索发现了一组可在二维最近邻量子比特连接架构上实现的qLDPC码（Bunny codes），并对其码率、距离和逻辑错误率进行了系统评估。

Result: Bunny codes在周期边界条件下，权重为6的稳定子码的码率约为toric码的3倍；开放边界条件下约为旋转surface码的2倍。电路级仿真显示，部分Bunny codes的逻辑错误率比相同码率的toric码低一个数量级。

Conclusion: 高性能量子纠错可以通过扩展门集而非长程耦合器实现，从而显著降低硬件复杂度。

Abstract: Drawing on advances in superconducting qubit control schemes that unlock enriched native gate sets at the hardware level, we systematically examine how harnessing this enlarged physical two-qubit gate pool -- specifically CNOT and CXSWAP -- streamlines syndrome extraction for certain qLDPC codes with nonlocal stabilizers. Through an exhaustive search, we discover a set of qLDPC codes with various stabilizer weights and distances that can be implemented on the two-dimensional nearest-neighbor qubit connectivity native to superconducting hardware while achieving performance equivalent to that of the direct CNOT implementation requiring long-range interactions. We refer to those codes as Bunny codes. Across all code distances we examine, the best Bunny codes with weight-6 stabilizers in periodic boundary conditions have a code rate approximately $3\times$ that of the toric code; when converted to open boundary conditions, they retain an approximately $2\times$ code rate advantage over the rotated surface code. In circuit-level simulation, we find that some Bunny codes exhibit logical error rates an order of magnitude lower than toric codes with comparable code rates. Our results demonstrate that high-performance quantum error correction can be achieved using an expanded gate set rather than long-range couplers, thereby significantly reducing hardware complexity.

</details>


### [102] [Adversarial Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Eavesdropping in BB84 Quantum Key Distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22962)
*Ramon Jose C. Bagunu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本论文研究将Eve建模为使用强化学习（Q-Learning、SARSA、Double Q-Learning）的自适应学习代理，在BB84 QKD协议中进行窃听。相比非自适应分析基线，Q-Learning在信道噪声1%时将检测率从99.4%降至0.28%，同时每轮提取约10.5个正确比特，实现了355倍的检测率降低。论文还发现代理自发学会了"终局爆发"攻击模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统BB84 QKD安全性分析假设Eve以固定的、分析优化的速率进行攻击，忽略了攻击者可能具备学习能力和自适应策略的现实可能性。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索当Eve作为学习代理时能否实现更隐蔽、更高效的窃听。

Method: 使用经典强化学习算法（表格型Q-Learning、SARSA、Double Q-Learning），将窃听问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程。代理基于量子比特错误率（QBER）反馈，逐量子比特决定是否拦截或放行。实验涵盖三种信道噪声水平（1%、3%、5%），使用5个独立随机种子验证，每个条件45次训练运行，每次10000轮。

Result: Q-Learning在1%信道噪声下将检测率从99.4%降至0.28%±0.27%，提取约10.5个正确比特/轮，实现355倍检测率降低（p=0.020，Mann-Whitney U检验）。代理自发学会终局爆发（end-game burst）策略——在最后块突然提升攻击率，该行为在随机化检查点间隔后消失，但隐身性能无显著变化。

Conclusion: 自适应学习代理能显著降低BB84窃听检测率，表明量子密钥分发安全性评估中应纳入自适应攻击者的基线，传统固定速率攻击假设可能低估实际安全风险。

Abstract: BB84 quantum key distribution derives its security from a physical guarantee that any eavesdropper disturbs the channel in a statistically detectable way. Prior work evaluates this by assuming Eve attacks at a fixed, analytically optimized rate. We examine what happens when Eve is modeled instead as a learning agent. Classical reinforcement learning is used, specifically tabular Q-Learning, SARSA, and Double Q-Learning, to adaptive BB84 eavesdropping. This formulates the attacker's decision as a Markov Decision Process where the agent observes Quantum Bit Error Rate (QBER) feedback and decides, qubit by qubit, whether to intercept or pass. Experiments span three channel noise levels ($μ_{ch}\in\{1\%,3\%,5\%\}$) and are validated across five independent random seeds (45 training runs per condition, 10,000 episodes each). Against the best non-adaptive analytical baseline, Q-Learning reduces detection from $99.4\%$ to $0.28\%\pm0.27\%$ at $μ_{ch}=1\%$ while extracting approximately 10.5 correct bits per episode. This is a 355-fold reduction that is statistically significant ($p=0.020$, Mann-Whitney $U$ test). We also report the spontaneous emergence of an end-game burst, where agents independently learn to surge their attack rate at the final block. This exploit vanishes under randomized checkpoint intervals while stealth performance remains statistically indistinguishable. These results motivate the inclusion of adaptive adversary baselines in quantum cryptographic security evaluations.

</details>


### [103] [Satellite Mission Planning with Rydberg Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23045)
*Michel Nowak,Benjamin Marchand,Yassine Naghmouchi,Serge Rainjonneau,Wesley Coelho,Louis Vignoli,Louis-Paul Henry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用基于冷原子（里德伯原子）的量子处理单元（QPU）解决地球观测卫星任务规划问题。通过将卫星规划问题转化为最大独立集问题，并分别采用图方法和QUBO框架进行求解，实验表明QUBO方法更为有效，论证了QPU在该问题上的潜在应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷原子量子计算机在优化领域具有处理经典计算难以解决的大量配置的潜力；日常卫星任务调度涉及大量单元和高密度目标，需要高效的规划方案。

Method: 将卫星任务规划问题形式化为最大独立集（MIS）问题，适配冷原子友好的量子计算框架；分别采用基于图的方法和QUBO（二次无约束二元优化）框架在里德伯原子QPU上求解。

Result: QUBO方法比基于图的方法更为相关和有效，论文通过数值实验对QUBO方法进行了更深入的探索。

Conclusion: 论证了在操作情境下使用QPU解决卫星任务规划问题的潜在实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum computers relying on cold atoms are being built and promise a high flexibility in the way information in encoded into the physical system. In particular, the analog mode is spiking interest in the field of optimization as a classically intractable number of configurations can be tackled. In this work, we investigate a problem that requires every-day scheduling of critical tasks involving a large number of actors. Namely, fixing the planning for a Earth Observation satellite fleet composed of several of units exposed to a high density of targets to be scanned. We explore numerical schemes that convert the formulated problem into a cold-atoms friendly setup. We begin by a naive formulation of the Satellite Mission Planning problem without taking account for the agility of the satellites. We then extend the problem to take it into account based on the literature. By formulating the planning problem as a Maximum Independent Set problem, we are able to solve the problem with a QPU based on Rydberg atoms. We explore two ways of solving the MIS problem on the QPU, one relying on the graphs and on the Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization Framework (QUBO). We show that the QUBO methodology is the most relevant and explore it more deeply with numerical experiments. We conclude on the potential utility of using a QPU to solve the Satellite Mission Planning problem in an operational context.

</details>


### [104] [An Efficient and Perfect Secret Sharing Scheme on a Class of Non-Maximal Quantum Access Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23082)
*Lei Li,Zhi Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对非最大量子访问结构下的量子秘密共享方案存在量子资源消耗高、安全性与效率难以平衡的问题，研究了三超边超星结构上的高效完美量子秘密共享方案构建方法，通过定义代表元访问结构并借助经典秘密共享方案，实现了量子资源轻量化部署，提升了安全性与资源利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前量子秘密共享方案在非最大量子访问结构下存在量子资源消耗高、安全性与效率难以平衡的问题，亟需探索更高效的方案构建方法。

Method: 首先刻画非最大QAS中的禁止集和中间集，给出纯态编码可实现的非最大QAS的表征；然后定义三超边超星的代元QAS作为其子结构；最后结合经典秘密共享方案，提出基于该超星QAS的通用、高效、完美的量子秘密共享方案。

Result: 提出了基于三超边超星QAS的高效完美量子秘密共享方案，通过轻量化量子资源部署降低了量子态制备与分发的难度，提升了安全性和资源利用效率。

Conclusion: 本文为非最大量子访问结构下的量子秘密共享提供了新的构建思路，所提方案在降低资源消耗的同时保证了安全性和效率。

Abstract: Quantum secret sharing is one of the core security technologies in the field of quantum communication. Aiming at the problems of high quantum resource consumption and the difficulty in balancing security and efficiency existing in current quantum secret sharing schemes under non-maximal quantum access structure(QAS), this paper focuses on the judgment criteria of non-maximal QAS and the construction methods of efficient and perfect quantum secret sharing schemes on a kind of non-maximal QAS, i.e. hyperstar with three hyperedges. Firstly, we characterizes the forbidden sets and intermediate sets within this non-maximal QAS, and provides a characterization for non-maximal QAS realizable by pure-state encoding. Secondly, the representative element QAS of hyperstar with three hyperedges is defined, which serves as a substructure of this hyperstar QAS, and belongs to non-maximal QAS. Furthermore, we propose a universal, efficient, and perfect quantum secret sharing scheme based on this hyperstar QAS using corresponding classical secret sharing scheme as an auxiliary. By deploying lightweight quantum resources to representative element access structures, the proposed scheme reduces the difficulty in quantum state preparation and distribution, and enhances its security and resource utilization efficiency.

</details>


### [105] [Understanding Squeezed States of Light Through Wigner's Phase-Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23102)
*Sibel Baskal,Marilyn E. Noz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了量子力学中相空间方法的发展，重点讨论了Wigner相空间分布函数、压缩态、纠缠以及耦合谐振子等主题，并运用洛伦兹群、辛群等对称性群进行分析，最后通过庞加莱球重新表述探讨了光学场的退相干问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从经典物理到量子力学的转变中，相空间概念起到了重要辅助作用，本文旨在系统阐述相空间图像在量子力学（尤其是量子光学）中的应用与价值。

Method: 通过定义Wigner相空间分布函数，从密度矩阵出发，讨论谐振子、相干态、单模双模压缩态及压缩真空态的相空间表示；利用洛伦兹群、二维和四维辛群、欧几里得群等对称性群进行分析；通过庞加莱球重新表述来研究光学场的退相干问题。

Result: 表明相空间图像已成为量子光学领域的重要科学语言；压缩态自然产生两模之间的纠缠；对称性群为理解量子系统提供了有力工具。

Conclusion: Wigner函数的简化形式在量子力学多个分支中具有广泛应用，相空间方法为量子光学及相关领域提供了重要的理论框架。

Abstract: This paper starts with the transition from classical physics to quantum mechanics which was greatly aided by the concept of phase space. The role of canonical transformations in quantum mechanics is addressed. The Wigner phase-space distribution function is then defined which arises from the formulation of the density matrix, followed by the harmonic oscillator in phase space. Coherent and one- and two-mode squeezed states of light as well as the squeezed vacuum are discussed in the phase-space picture. Attention is also drawn to the fact that squeezed states naturally generate entanglement between the two-modes. Coupled harmonic oscillators are also elucidated in connection with the Wigner phase space. It will be noted that the phase-space picture of quantum mechanics has become an important scientific language for the rapidly expanding field of quantum optics. Here, we mainly focus on the simplest form of the Wigner function, which finds application in many branches of quantum mechanics. We make use of several symmetry groups such as Lorentz groups, the symplectic group in two and four dimensions, and the Euclidean group. The decoherence problem of an optical field is examined through a reformulation of the Poincaré sphere as a further illustration of the density matrix.

</details>


### [106] [Entropic Uncertainty Relations for Mutually Unbiased Operator Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23185)
*Jesni Shamsul Shaari,Stefano Mancini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在希尔伯特-施密特算子空间中建立了算子框架形式的熵不确定关系，通过Riesz-Thorin插值推导了一般连续索引算子框架的熵不确定关系，并识别了一类通过常数模迹重叠定义的无偏算子框架，在适当结构条件下导出了更强的Hirschman-Beckner型熵不确定关系，通过Weyl位移算子和Wigner核等实例将熵不确定关系从测量结果推广到算子表示本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将熵不确定关系从传统的测量结果层面推广到算子表示层面，建立更一般的算子框架理论框架。

Method: 采用算子框架形式化方法，结合Riesz-Thorin插值定理推导一般熵不确定关系；通过定义常数模迹重叠的无偏算子框架，利用双线性傅里叶变换建立更强的Hirschman-Beckner型关系。

Result: 推导了一般连续索引算子框架的熵不确定关系，发现了无偏算子框架类并建立了更强的熵不确定关系，通过Weyl位移算子、Wigner核以及位置-动量本征态生成的笛卡尔二进框架等实例验证了理论。

Conclusion: 该工作将熵不确定关系从测量结果扩展到算子表示领域，统一了连续变量傅里叶对偶性，为量子信息理论提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: We develop an operator-frame formulation of entropic uncertainty relations in the Hilbert-Schmidt space of operators. For general continuous indexed operator frames, we derive an entropic uncertainty relation for the associated coefficient distributions by combining endpoint norm estimates with Riesz-Thorin interpolation. We then identify a distinguished class of mutually unbiased operator frames, defined through constant-modulus trace overlaps. Under suitable structural conditions, the corresponding coefficient amplitudes are related by a bilinear Fourier transform, leading to a stronger Hirschman-Beckner-type entropic uncertainty relation. As canonical realizations, we consider Weyl displacement operators and Wigner kernels, as well as Cartesian dyadic frames generated by position and momentum eigenstates. These examples recover familiar continuous-variable Fourier dualities while extending entropic uncertainty relations beyond measurement outcomes to operator representations themselves.

</details>


### [107] [Efficient Energy-Constrained Semi-Device-Independent QRNG with an Integrated Heterodyne Receiver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23231)
*Mattia Sabatini,Carles Roch i Carceller,Tommaso Bertapelle,Andrea Peri,Giulio Gualandi,Matías Rubén Bolaños,Yoann Piétri,Giacomo Corrielli,Paolo Villoresi,Giuseppe Vallone,Roberto Osellame,Armin Tavakoli,Marco Avesani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实验展示了一种基于光子数约束的半设备无关量子随机数生成器，采用四态相干态星座与零差探测，实现了每测量0.223比特的可认证Shannon熵下界，这是连续变量半设备无关QRNG迄今报道的最高值，结合熵累积方法无需假设独立同分布即可进行有限尺寸随机性认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半设备无关QRNG框架兼具强安全性保证和高随机数生成速率，且仅依赖简化且实用的物理假设。基于光子数约束的方案特别适合光子学实现，但其实验验证尚待完善，需要在实际系统中实现并量化可认证的随机性。

Method: 采用半定松弛技术直接计算可认证Shannon熵下界；结合熵累积方法实现有限尺寸随机性认证（无需i.i.d.假设）；使用相位空间中对称分布的四态相干态星座，通过零差探测进行测量；采用低损耗集成光子学零差接收器与商用组件组装的简单发射器。

Result: 每测量认证0.223比特的Shannon熵下界，这是连续变量半设备无关QRNG迄今报道的最高值。

Conclusion: 该方案结合了集成光子学接收器与商用发射器，提供了实用且高速的半设备无关随机数生成架构，为实际部署半设备无关QRNG奠定了实验基础。

Abstract: Semi-device-independent QRNG frameworks represent a particularly attractive approach, combining strong security guarantees with high randomness generation rates while relying only on reduced and practical physical assumptions. A recently proposed approach based on photon-number constraints is particularly suited to photonic implementations, where these assumptions can be easily assessed experimentally. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a quantum random number generator within this framework, enabling the direct computation of lower bounds on the certifiable Shannon entropy via semidefinite relaxation techniques. When combined with entropy accumulation methods, this approach enables finite-size randomness certification without assuming independent and identically distributed rounds. We realize the protocol using a four-state coherent-state constellation symmetrically distributed in phase space and measured by heterodyne detection, certifying 0.223 bit per measurement, which is the highest value reported to date for a continuous-variable semi-device-independent QRNG. The implementation combines a low-loss integrated photonic heterodyne receiver with a simple transmitter assembled from commercial components, providing a practical and high-speed architecture for semi-device-independent randomness generation.

</details>


### [108] [Quantum Key Distribution Without Shared Reference Frame Under Unital Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23239)
*Junaid ur Rehman,Shehbaz Tariq,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在不共享参考系且信道未知的静态单量子比特幺正信道下，通过构建泡利转移矩阵或顺序基匹配两种方法实现量子密钥分发，并证明在此条件下BB84和六态协议的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际量子密钥分发（如卫星QKD）中，通信双方无法建立全局共享参考系（如相对运动与旋转），同时信道噪声未知。现有协议通常假设已知参考系或信道特性，但实际场景中两者均未知，导致密钥生成率受限甚至不可行。

Method: 提出两种方法：1）构建泡利转移矩阵（PTM）描述信道，通过将缺少共享参考系的影响吸收到信道定义中，无需共享参考系即可识别PTM的奇异向量作为最优信号态的布洛赫向量，从而在优化局部基下将关联等效为泡利信道；2）顺序基匹配（SBM）方法，逐步识别信道优化的局部基以实现QKD。

Result: 两种方法在无共享参考系和未知单量子比特幺正信道下均可实现QKD，且产生相同的有效密钥交换速率；证明在此条件下BB84和六态QKD协议具有最优性。

Conclusion: 无需全局共享参考系和信道先验知识，通过PTM或SBM方法可实现鲁棒的QKD，适用于卫星通信等实际场景，并确认了标准协议的最优性。

Abstract: We consider a general and practical scenario of quantum key distribution (QKD) over an unknown, stationary, unital qubit channel. Furthermore, due to practical limitations, e.g., relative movement and rotation of communicating parties, a global shared reference frame cannot be established. This scenario can routinely appear in satellite QKD. We propose two methods to overcome the physical qubit noise and the lack of shared reference frame. The first proposed approach involves constructing the Pauli transfer matrix (PTM) description of the channel, which we achieve without requiring a shared reference frame, by absorbing the lack of shared reference frame in the channel definition. This is followed by the identification of singular vectors of PTM as the Bloch vectors for optimal signal states. In the optimized local bases, the resulting correlations are equivalent, up to outcome relabeling, to those of a Pauli channel, allowing us to show the optimality of the BB84 and six-state QKD protocols under these conditions. The second approach, called the sequential basis matching (SBM) involves sequentially identifying the channel-optimized local bases that enable QKD. We show that both of these approaches result in the same effective key exchange rate for QKD.

</details>


### [109] [Tuning Quantum MPS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23262)
*Anna Leonteva,Maxime Outteryck,Guido Masella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种两阶段框架，用于自动选择量子MPS模拟的超参数：第一阶段通过CMA-ES进行离线单目标优化并构建数据库，第二阶段基于电路特征训练混合排序模型推荐配置。实验表明离线优化优于默认设置，但学习到的预测器性能受电路族和规模影响，且低于离线最优值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPS方法是经典模拟量子电路的最有效方法之一，但其实际性能严重依赖于模拟器的超参数，默认设置往往不是最优的，因此需要自动化的超参数选择方法。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段在保真度约束下进行离线单目标CMA-ES优化，构建电路-配置-性能评估数据库；第二阶段定义静态电路特征捕捉MPS相关结构属性，训练电路感知的混合排序模型来推荐配置。采用留一电路族和基于规模的验证方法评估。

Result: 离线优化通常能改善默认设置，但增益幅度强烈依赖于后端、电路族和电路规模；学习到的预测器能恢复部分增益，基于规模的验证优于基于电路族的迁移性能，但整体仍低于离线最优值。

Conclusion: 自动超参数选择对量子MPS模拟有实际价值，但电路族间的迁移性存在挑战，需进一步改进跨族泛化能力。

Abstract: Matrix Product State (MPS) methods are among the most effective approaches for the classical simulation of quantum circuits, but their practical performance depends strongly on simulator hyperparameters, and default settings are often suboptimal. In this work, we propose a two-stage framework for automatic hyperparameter selection for quantum MPS simulation. In the first stage, we perform offline single-objective CMA-ES optimization under a fidelity constraint and construct a database of circuit--configuration--performance evaluations. In the second stage, we define a set of static circuit features designed to capture MPS-relevant structural properties and train a circuit-aware hybrid ranking model to recommend configurations for different quantum circuits. We evaluate the approach on multiple scalable circuit families using leave-one-family-out and size-based validation. The results show that offline optimization often improves over default settings, although the magnitude of the gain depends strongly on the backend, circuit family, and circuit scale. The learned predictor recovers a meaningful fraction of this gain, with better performance under size-based validation than under family-based transfer, but generally remains below the offline optimum.

</details>


### [110] [Fast quantum-state transfer in Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains beyond the noninteracting regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23324)
*François Impens,David Guéry-Odelin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在Su-Schrieffer-Heeger链中通过可调次近邻跳跃相位实现相互作用拓扑系统的快速绝热捷径，在平均场和非线性条件下实现高保真度边缘态转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 绝热捷径在单粒子体系中已实现拓扑边缘态快速转移，但将其推广到相互作用系统时受到非线性相位累积的阻碍，亟需解决这一难题。

Method: 通过使次近邻跳跃相位可调，在平均场框架下构造精确非线性捷径：一个跳跃正交分量将系统保持在瞬时暗态轨迹上，正交的另一分量抵消相互作用诱导的自相位调制。随后在多体希尔伯特空间直接优化转移过程。

Result: 平均场协议是非微扰的，对全Bose-Hubbard动力学有效但保真度低于1，揭示了超越乘积态图像的真实多体修正。在多体希尔伯特空间优化后，复相位可调次近邻跳跃可恢复近完美保真度。

Conclusion: 跳跃相位不仅是技术上的改进，更是实现相互作用拓扑系统中快速高保真传输的关键控制资源。

Abstract: Shortcuts to adiabaticity have made topological edge-state transfer fast in the single-particle regime, but their extension to interacting systems is obstructed by nonlinear phase accumulation. We show that this obstruction can be removed in Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chains by making the next-nearest-neighbor shortcut hopping phase tunable. In the mean-field regime, this yields an exact nonlinear shortcut: one hopping quadrature keeps the state on the instantaneous dark-state trajectory, while the orthogonal quadrature cancels the interaction-induced self-phase modulation. The resulting protocol is nonperturbative in the mean-field interaction strength. When applied to the full Bose-Hubbard dynamics, the mean-field shortcut remains beneficial but saturates below unit fidelity, exposing genuinely many-body corrections beyond the product-state picture. We then optimize the transfer directly in the many-body Hilbert space and find that complex, phase-tunable next-nearest-neighbor hoppings recover near-perfect fidelity. Our results show that hopping phases are not merely a technical refinement, but a key control resource for fast and high-fidelity transport in interacting topological systems.

</details>


### [111] [Bounding Classical and Quantum Correlations in Bayesian Networks with Quasiprobabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23372)
*Paul Becsi,Matty J. Hoban*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究贝叶斯网络中的非经典相关性，提出用准概率（quasiprobabilities）替代经典概率来建模未观测节点，并证明在广泛网络类别中，准概率模型能恢复嵌套马尔可夫模型（nested Markov model）对应的相关集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子与经典相关性在贝叶斯网络中的界限，寻求对量子相关性的外部近似方法。

Method: 提出准概率贝叶斯网络模型（将未观测节点的概率分布替换为满足归一化但不要求非负性的准概率），并利用张量网络分解建立联系，证明该模型在广泛网络类别中能再现所有非信号相关性，进而推测准集能恢复嵌套马尔可夫模型。

Result: 证明了准概率模型在广泛网络类别中可产生所有非信号相关性；推测准集能恢复嵌套马尔可夫模型。

Conclusion: 准概率模型为理解贝叶斯网络中的非经典相关性提供了新视角，张量网络分解的连接可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Bell's theorem reveals that quantum theory is in tension with classical causal reasoning and, in particular, the notion of local causality. This is now understood as a particular example of non-classicality in the study of correlations in (Bayesian) networks with both unobserved and observed nodes: the correlations are probability distributions over the observed nodes. There is a great deal of work aiming to understand the bounds on quantum and classical correlations in such networks and one approach is to consider outer approximations to the former. Along these lines, we consider quasiprobabilistic models for Bayesian networks, which can be seen as classical models but the probability distributions involving unobserved nodes are "replaced" with quasiprobabilities that respect normalisation but not positivity. We denote the set of correlations resulting from these models as the quasi set. Such models have a history in the study of Bell-type non-classicality where it has been shown that they can produce all non-signalling correlations. We show a generalisation of this result for a broad class of networks, which motivates a conjecture that the quasi set recovers the so called nested Markov model. Our work utilises a connection to tensor network decompositions, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [112] [On the cryptographic potential of single-qubit rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23392)
*Alex B. Grilo,Lucas Hanouz,Anne Marin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种可组合安全构造，使得在量子密码协议中，原本需要可信量子比特制备或测量的参与方可以将这些任务委托给不可信提供者，仅需依赖可信单量子比特旋转设备。第一个构造实现单量子比特测量，通用适用；第二个构造处理量子比特制备，依赖特定假设但大多数常见协议自然满足。结果正式验证了Qline架构支持多种单量子比特协议的通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子通信中，密码协议通常要求用户拥有可信的量子比特源或探测器。最近研究表明，在Qline架构上，仅具备单量子比特旋转能力的参与方也能等效执行多种协议。本文旨在进一步扩展这一思路，将可信量子比特制备和测量任务委托给不可信提供者，从而降低对用户设备的要求。

Method: 提出两种可组合安全构造：(1) 实现单量子比特测量的通用构造，适用于任何上下文；(2) 处理量子比特制备的构造，依赖于对底层协议的特定假设，但这些假设在大多数常见量子密码协议中天然满足。

Result: 成功证明了在大多数量子密码协议中，传统上需要可信量子比特制备或测量的参与方可以将这些任务委托给不可信提供者，仅需依赖可信单量子比特旋转设备。正式验证了Qline架构作为支持广泛单量子比特协议的通用平台的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为Qline架构提供了形式化安全验证，表明其作为一种多功能架构，能够支持广泛的单量子比特协议。这一结果显著降低了对量子密码协议用户端设备的要求，推动了量子通信的实际应用。

Abstract: In the domain of quantum communication, cryptographic protocols often require users to have access to trusted qubit sources or detectors. Recently, it was shown that on an architecture called the Qline, several protocols can equivalently be performed by parties capable only of single-qubit rotations. In this work, we introduce two composably secure constructions that together show how in most quantum cryptographic protocols, parties traditionally required to perform trusted qubit preparation or measurement can delegate these tasks to an untrusted provider and instead rely on a trusted single-qubit rotation device. Our first construction implements single-qubit measurement and is universally applicable across any context. In contrast, our second construction, which addresses qubit preparation, relies on specific assumptions regarding the underlying protocol. We show, however, that these assumptions are inherently satisfied by the vast majority of common quantum cryptographic protocols. A notable consequence of our results is the formal validation of the Qline as a versatile architecture capable of supporting a wide range of single-qubit protocols.

</details>


### [113] [Quantum Convolutional Neural Networks for Groundwater Heat Plume Prediction: A Surrogate Modeling Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23411)
*Danyal Maheshwari,Julia Pelzer,Miriam Schulte*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探索了量子卷积神经网络（QCNN）作为慕尼黑地区地下水热泵温度预测的替代模型，在理想模拟器、噪声模拟器、127量子比特Kyiv处理器及纠错增强硬件上评估性能，结果表明QCNN在模拟器上表现稳健，在纠错硬件上有明显提升，虽不及经典神经网络，但展示了量子替代建模的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用量子机器学习方法建模复杂环境系统（地下水热羽流动力学），以克服经典方法在高维数据建模中的局限性，并探索量子硬件在环境工程领域的实际应用可行性。

Method: 采用量子卷积神经网络（QCNN），包含量子卷积层、量子池化层和全连接量子读出阶段；通过参数化量子电路（旋转门+测量驱动解码）实现卷积和池化操作，使用哈密顿量启发的特征编码方案制备输入态；将高维模拟输出降维为紧凑参数集作为训练目标。

Result: QCNN在理想模拟器和噪声模拟器上表现稳定，在纠错增强的IBM Kyiv 127量子比特硬件上性能有显著提升；经典神经网络仍具有最高预测精度，但QCNN展现了竞争性表现。

Conclusion: 量子增强替代建模是未来地下水温度预测的有前景方向，随着量子硬件和纠错技术的成熟，其性能有望进一步提升。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning methods are increasingly explored for modeling complex environmental systems, including groundwater heat plume dynamics. In this work, we explore a Quantum Convolutional Neural Network (QCNN) as a surrogate model for predicting temperature variations in groundwater induced by geothermal heat pumps in the city of Munich. To comply with the scalability constraints of current quantum hardware, the original high-dimensional simulation output is reduced to a compact set of representative parameters that serve as training targets for the surrogate. The proposed QCNN architecture consists of a quantum convolutional layer, a quantum pooling layer, and a fully connected quantum readout stage. Convolution and pooling operations are realized via parameterized quantum circuits based on rotational gates and measurement-driven decoding, while a Hamiltonian-inspired feature-encoding scheme is used to prepare informative input states on the quantum device. We evaluate the QCNN across multiple execution backends, including an ideal statevector simulator, a noisy simulator, IBM's 127-qubit Kyiv quantum processor, and the same hardware augmented with advanced error-mitigation techniques. Realistic noise models are employed to approximate device behavior and to assess the impact of mitigation strategies. Model performance is benchmarked using mean squared error (MSE) on both training and testing sets. The results show that, although classical neural networks still achieve the highest predictive accuracy, the QCNN attains competitive and consistent performance on simulators and exhibits noticeable improvement under error-mitigated hardware conditions. These findings indicate that quantum-enhanced surrogate modeling is a promising direction for future groundwater temperature prediction as quantum hardware and error-mitigation techniques continue to mature.

</details>


### [114] [Dissipative preparation of Laughlin-like states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23451)
*Tong Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于局域损耗和泵浦通道的纯耗散协议，用于制备填充因子为1/3的Laughlin-like态（可推广至1/M填充态）。证明该态是Lindbladians的精确稳态，可从任意初态到达，且Lindleadian能隙随系统尺寸增大有下界。通过缓慢调制泵浦通道实现了Laughlin-like态的绝热泵浦，为近期量子模拟器上制备和操控分数量子霍尔态提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数量子霍尔态是强关联量子物质的核心范例和拓扑量子计算的关键平台，但现有制备方案在实验上具有较高难度。因此，需要寻找一种更可行、对初始条件不敏感且能扩展到较大系统的制备方案。

Method: 提出纯耗散协议，利用局域损耗和泵浦通道构建Lindblad主方程，使Laughlin-like态成为其唯一稳态；通过分析Lindbladian能隙的下界保证收敛性；引入绝热调制泵浦通道实现态的操控。

Result: Laughlin-like态是Lindbladians的精确稳态，可从任意初始态达到；能隙随系统尺寸增大有非零下界（即不关闭）；成功演示了通过缓慢调制泵浦通道实现Laughlin-like态的绝热泵浦。

Conclusion: 该工作为在近期量子模拟器上制备和操控分数量子霍尔态开辟了一条可行的道路，有助于推动拓扑量子计算和强关联量子物质研究的发展。

Abstract: Fractional quantum Hall (FQH) states are a central paradigm of strongly correlated quantum matter and a key platform for topological quantum computation. Here, we propose a purely dissipative protocol based on local loss and pump channels for preparing Laughlin-like states at filling $1/3$, with a possible extension to other $1/M$ filling states. We show that Laughlin-like states are the exact steady states of the Lindbladian and can be reached from arbitrary initial states. We find that the Lindbladian gap bounded from below with increasing system size. We further demonstrate adiabatic pumping of a Laughlin-like state through slow modulation of the pump channels during the evolution. Our work opens a feasible route to preparing and manipulating FQH states on near-term quantum simulators.

</details>


### [115] [Improved State Readout in NV Centers using Regression Models and Rabi Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23454)
*Fritz Haltenberger,Manpreet Singh Jattana*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的回归方法，利用增强的动态荧光信号（如Rabi振荡期间测量）替代传统标量校准，显著提升了氮空位（NV）中心室温下电子自旋态读取的保真度；线性脊回归性能接近非线性模型，表明简单模型即可捕获荧光信号与态布居之间的映射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于对比度的标量校准方法将NV中心的动态荧光行为简化为单一数值并独立计算每个量子态的布居数，导致读取保真度受限。

Method: 使用回归模型（线性脊回归、核方法等）将实验测量的荧光信号映射到理想模拟的态布居数；通过在诱导的Rabi振荡期间进行测量来增强荧光信号的信息含量。

Result: 包含动态荧光信号显著降低了多种测试模型的状态读取误差；线性脊回归性能几乎与非线性核模型相当。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动方法在设定条件下实现了高于传统校准的保真度，为固态量子寄存器中的高保真状态读取提供了稳健的替代方案。

Abstract: Readout of state populations in nitrogen-vacancy centers from fluorescence measurements at room-temperature is routinely achieved via contrast-based calibration. The fidelities achieved by this conventional approach are limited by reducing the dynamical fluorescence behaviour of the NV center to a scalar value, and calculating the population of each possible state independently. To address these limitations, we use regression models trained on experimental data to map the fluorescence signals onto ideal simulated populations. Additionally, we enhance the informational content of the fluorescence signals by performing measurements during induced Rabi oscillations. Our results demonstrate that including these dynamical signals significantly reduces state readout errors across multiple tested models. Notably, linear ridge regression performs nearly on par with a non-linear kernel-based model, showing that simple models already capture the relevant mapping between the enhanced fluorescence signals and the underlying state populations. This data-driven approach provides a robust alternative that achieves higher fidelities than conventional calibration in our setting, paving the way for high-fidelity state readout in solid-state quantum registers.

</details>


### [116] [Prethermal rotating-frame solid echo in a dipolar nuclear-spin network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23467)
*Quentin Reynard-Feytis,William Beatrez,Leo Joon Il Moon,Emanuel Druga,Ashok Ajoy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在超极化金刚石核自旋网络中实现了旋转框架下的Floquet预热化固体回波，并通过解析和数值模拟表明该回波源于Floquet微运动在不同算符子空间之间转移相干性，回波包络以拉伸指数衰减，特征时间T₂'≈13 ms，为高吞吐量光谱学和量子传感提供了新平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用Floquet预热化实现哈密顿量工程和新的动力学探测手段，并将经典回波物理拓展到预热旋转框架中。

Method: 使用脉冲自旋锁定驱动的超极化偶极耦合¹³C核自旋网络，进入旋转框架预热平台，进行周期分辨的感应读出，并通过解析论证和玩具模型模拟解释回波机制。

Result: 观察到旋转框架固体回波：延迟τ后磁化强度在2τ时刻恢复，最大振幅在α≈π/2附近；回波包络以拉伸指数衰减，特征时间T₂'≈13 ms。

Conclusion: 回波由Floquet微运动将相干性在不同算符子空间之间转移导致，仅有部分多体退相动力学被y脉冲反转；预热自旋系综可用于高吞吐量光谱学、哈密顿量工程和长时量子传感。

Abstract: Floquet prethermalization can endow interacting quantum solids with long-lived, approximately conserved quantities, enabling Hamiltonian engineering and new dynamical probes. Using a hyperpolarized network of dipolar-coupled $^{13}$C nuclear spins in diamond driven by pulsed spin-locking, we access a rotating-frame prethermal plateau with quasi-conserved transverse magnetization and cycle-resolved inductive readout. Within this prethermal manifold we observe a robust \emph{rotating-frame solid echo}: after an apparent decay of the rotating-frame free-induction signal over a delay $τ$, the magnetization revives at time $2τ$ following a single $(α)_y$ pulse, with maximum amplitude near $α\simeqπ/2$. The echo envelope decays as a stretched exponential with characteristic time $T_2'\approx 13\,$ms. Analytical arguments and toy-model simulations attribute the revival to Floquet micromotion that transfers coherences between operator subspaces, so that only a subset of the many-body dephasing dynamics is inverted by the $y$ pulse. These results translate classic echo physics into the prethermal rotating frame and point to continuously interrogated prethermal spin ensembles as a versatile platform for high-throughput spectroscopy, Hamiltonian engineering, and long-duration quantum sensing.

</details>


### [117] [How Stark units enter SIC overlaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23535)
*Ingemar Bengtsson,Gary McConnell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究SIC-POVM中向量间标量积（重叠单元）的代数性质，发现它们总是由来自基域最大整数环的射线类域中Stark单位平方根的整数幂乘积构成，非最小情况下涉及这些射线类域的格结构，并在特定维度中某些重叠单元等于±1，这一现象可由射线类域的特殊性质解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示SIC-POVM重叠单元的代数结构，理解其与Stark单位和射线类域之间的深层联系，并解释非最小情况下和特定维度中出现的特殊现象。

Method: 结合精确代数论证与数值证据，分析重叠单元与射线类域中Stark单位平方根的关系，研究非最小情况下的射线类域格结构，并通过射线类域的特殊性质推导特定维度中重叠单元等于±1的结论。

Result: 重叠单元总是由基域最大整数环所附射线类域中Stark单位平方根的整数幂乘积构成；非最小情况下涉及多个射线类域形成的格结构；在每第二个维度（按特定方式计数）中，某些重叠单元等于±1，这可由射线类域的特殊性质导出。

Conclusion: 本研究的发现补充并支持了重叠单元可直接从Shintani-Faddeev模余环计算的观点，揭示了SIC-POVM与代数数论中Stark单位和射线类域之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: It has been observed that the mutual scalar products of the vectors in a SIC-POVM are given by algebraic units, and at least in some cases by square roots of Stark units. The full picture is somewhat more complicated, especially if non-minimal SIC-POVMs are considered. We present a mixture of exact and numerical evidence suggesting that the overlap units are always products of integral powers of square roots of Stark units from ray class fields all of which are attached to the maximal ring of integers in the base field. In the non-minimal case a lattice of such ray class fields is involved. In every second dimension (counted in a certain way) some of the overlap units equal $\pm 1$, and we show that this follows from a special property of the ray class fields. Our observations are complementary to but consistent with the claim that the overlap units can be calculated directly from the Shintani--Faddeev modular cocycle.

</details>


### [118] [Structure-Aware Variance Reduction for Unbiased Randomized Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23544)
*Joshua W. Dai,Fredrik Hasselgren,Chusei Kiumi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出连续时间演化概率角度插值（continuous TE-PAI）方法，利用经典方差缩减技术消除随机哈密顿量模拟中的离散化偏差，并在张量网络模拟中实现约80-96%的采样成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机哈密顿量模拟方法在系统偏差和采样开销之间存在权衡，如何在不引入额外偏差的前提下降低方差是核心问题。

Method: 提出连续TE-PAI协议，一种拟概率随机电路方法，消除了Trotter离散化误差；对方差进行经典计数分量和量子排序分量的规范分解，并针对性应用经典方差缩减技术。

Result: 连续TE-PAI在有限深度随机电路中消除Trotter误差；在n=30自旋链张量网络模拟中，偏差可忽略，误差降低约80%，采样成本降低约91%和96%。

Conclusion: 经典方差缩减技术可有效应用于随机哈密顿量模拟，在不引入额外偏差的前提下显著降低采样开销，尤其适用于可观测量和估计器定制化的统计策略。

Abstract: Randomized Hamiltonian simulation methods are often governed by a trade-off between systematic bias and sampling overhead. We study how classical variance-reduction techniques can be applied to such methods without changing their mean channel, and therefore without introducing additional bias. As a motivating unbiased estimator, we formulate continuous time-evolution probabilistic angle interpolation (continuous TE-PAI), a quasiprobabilistic random-circuit protocol whose remaining Monte Carlo error is purely statistical. Continuous TE-PAI removes Trotter discretization error with finite-depth random circuits, whereas deterministic Trotterization does so only in the infinite-depth limit. Further, in tensor-network simulations, we demonstrate that discretization error can cause an unphysical exponential growth in the bond dimension required for Trotterized simulations, whereas comparable-depth continuous TE-PAI circuits avoid this growth. We then show that the variance of randomized product-formula-based estimators admits a canonical decomposition into a classical counting component and a quantum ordering component such that the dominant simulation overhead results from the non-commutative parts of the Hamiltonian dynamics. Motivated by this decomposition, we achieve an $\approx70\%$ error-reduction using the counting-component for small systems whereas our tensor-network simulations of $n=30$ spin-chain dynamics use coarser statistics tailored to the observable and estimator attaining a negligible bias and a reduction of $\approx 80\%$ leading to $\approx91\%$ and $\approx96\%$ sampling-cost reductions, respectively.

</details>


### [119] [Genuine Global Kochen-Specker Contextuality as Classical Coordination Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23577)
*Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种新的量子关联经典模拟失效机制——"全局Kochen-Specker(KS)上下文性"，即局部子系统非上下文、多体块广义贝尔局域，但整体经验模型无全局非上下文隐变量解释。作者提出协调代价框架，将通信、记忆和局部计算视为经典模拟器维持全局经典解释的不同资源，引入协调比特、全局上下文覆盖数、任务族标度律及抽象提升定理，并通过极化-路径Hardy阻塞和后选择KCBS型任务等实例进行分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子关联的失败通常归因于两种机制：非低通信局域隐变量模型或单非上下文隐态无法解释所有相容测量上下文。本文探索第三种根本不同的机制——全局KS上下文性，旨在理解局部非上下文但整体非经典的量子关联结构。

Method: 提出协调代价框架，统一处理通信、记忆和局部计算三种资源；定义协调比特、全局上下文覆盖数、任务族标度律；证明抽象提升定理，将KS上下文种子族的经典模拟下界迁移到全局KS模型；通过极化-路径Hardy阻塞和后选择KCBS型任务进行实例验证。

Result: 建立了全局KS上下文性的形式化理论框架，包括资源量化指标与标度律，并成功通过两个具体量子任务展示了该框架的分析能力。

Conclusion: 全局KS上下文性是区别于局域隐变量模型和非上下文隐态失效的第三种量子关联经典模拟困难机制；协调代价框架为量化此类资源代价提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Classical simulations of quantum correlations can fail because no low-communication local hidden-variable model exists, or because no single noncontextual hidden state can explain all compatible measurement contexts. This manuscript studies a third regime: genuine global Kochen-Specker contextuality, where local subsystems are noncontextual and the tested multipartite blocks are generalized-Bell-local, but the whole empirical model admits no global noncontextual hidden-variable explanation. We propose a coordination-cost framework in which communication, memory, and local computation are treated as different ways for a classical simulator to maintain a global classical explanation from locally available information. We introduce coordination bits, global contextual covering numbers, scaling laws for task families, and an abstract lifting theorem showing how classical simulation lower bounds for KS-contextual seed families can be transferred to genuinely global-KS models. As worked examples, we analyze a polarization-path Hardy obstruction and postselected KCBS-type tasks.

</details>


### [120] [Random dimension reduction and learning symmetric properties of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23592)
*Angus Lowe,Xinyu Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种随机维度缩减过程，能在保持等距张量幂作用下不变性质的同时，同时降低多个可能不同量子态的维度。该方法用最大秩替代维度作为学习对称性质的样本复杂度中的参数，并通过Schur变换实现高效量子电路。研究表明，该方法结合完整态层析可提升状态间距离、保真度和相对熵估计的上界。通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构揭示其与随机纯化通道的深层联系，并完成了样本最优层析的端到端分析。最后证明不存在能同时纯化多个不同输入态的随机纯化通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低学习量子态对称性质时的样本复杂度，将维度依赖替换为最大秩依赖，并建立与随机纯化通道的理论联系。

Method: 提出随机维度缩减过程，利用Schur变换实现高效量子电路，通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构分析其与随机纯化通道的关系。

Result: 改进了状态间距离、保真度和相对熵估计的上界；完成了样本最优层析的端到端分析；证明了不存在能同时纯化多个不同输入态的随机纯化通道。

Conclusion: 随机维度缩减是一种与随机纯化相关但不同的新方法，能有效降低对称性质学习的样本复杂度，并在量子信息处理中有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: We introduce a procedure called random dimension reduction that simultaneously reduces the dimensions of many, potentially distinct quantum states while preserving properties invariant under the tensor power action of an isometry. This provides a black-box method to replace the dimension with the maximum rank in the sample complexity of learning symmetric properties, even those depending on multiple input states. We show that dimension reduction followed by full state tomography yields improved upper bounds for estimating distances, fidelities, and relative entropies between pairs of states. We also give an efficient quantum circuit implementation of the procedure using the Schur transform.
  Expressing the action of our procedure through the Choi-Jamiolkowski isomorphism reveals an intimate connection with the recently introduced random purification channel by Tang, Wright, and Zhandry. This perspective also completes an end-to-end analysis of sample-optimal tomography without requiring a reference to the Schur transform or Schur polynomials. Finally, we prove that there does not exist a random purification channel that simultaneously purifies copies of multiple, potentially different input states. Hence, random dimension reduction is related to, but distinct from, random purification.

</details>


### [121] [A quantum algorithm for one-shot signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23612)
*Gopikrishnan Muraleedharan,Minh Thuy Truc Pham,Vir Pathak,Thomas Gardner,Chuanqi Zhang,QPerfect,Gavin K. Brennen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种预混淆电路级别的高效一次性签名方案，基于随机仿射陪集与可穿刺伪随机函数实现，支持经典公钥/量子密钥对生成与经典签名验证，逻辑量子比特数为Θ(κ log(r) + n + l)，门复杂度为Θ(n³ + nl)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一种高效的一次性签名方案，具有预混淆电路级实现，可应用于委托签名、安全代币转移和公开可验证随机性等场景，并确保在经典和量子多项式时间攻击下的安全性。

Method: 方案分为密钥生成阶段（生成经典公钥/量子密钥对）和签名阶段（用量子密钥处理消息字符串生成经典签名）。核心方法是通过可穿刺伪随机函数的输出确定随机仿射陪集，制备该陪集元素的叠加态，并结合测试陪集成员资格的电路来实现签名。

Result: 方案无算法错误，签名可被经典验证者高效检验。逻辑量子比特数上界为Θ(κ log(r) + n + l)，门复杂度为Θ(n³ + nl)。提供了针对不同n的显式量子比特数和门数计数，并识别了为抵御经典和量子多项式时间攻击需要混淆的电路组件。

Conclusion: 该一次性签名方案在电路级别实现了高效性和可验证性，明确了安全所需的混淆组件，为后续量子安全签名方案的实用化提供了理论依据和复杂度基准。

Abstract: We provide a pre-obfuscation circuit-level implementation of an efficient one shot signature scheme, which has known applications to delegated signatures, secured token transfer, and publicly verifiable randomness. The algorithm consists of two stages: a key generation stage where a classical public key/quantum secret key pair is produced, and a signing stage where the quantum secret key is processed with a message string to produce a classical signature. There is no algorithmic error in the construction and the signed message can be efficiently checked by a classical verifier. Our scheme works by preparing a superposition over elements of a random affine coset determined by the output of a puncturable pseudorandom function, together with a circuit that tests coset membership. The logical qubit number scales like $Θ( κ\log(r) + n + l)$ and the gate complexity scales like $Θ(n^3 + nl)$, where $r$ is the public key size, $n+l$ is the signature size, $l$ is the message size, and $κ= Ω(n)$ is the cryptographic security parameter. We provide explicit qubit and gate counts for varying $n$ and identify the circuit components where obfuscation would be required for security against classical and quantum polynomial time attacks.

</details>


### [122] [Log-concavity and tunneling: adiabatic quantum optimization for convex functions (with a spike)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23614)
*Arthur Braida,Elie Bermot,Simon Apers*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了“汉明权重加尖峰”(HWS)问题的理论分析，建立了离散对数凹性作为基态的关键结构性质，并将其应用于更广泛的势能函数。作者证明了凸势能及某些具有局部极小值的势能下基态的对数凹性，并利用该性质将HWS的微扰分析从线性势推广到二次势，表明隧穿效应在凸势能加尖峰问题中具有更广泛的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子隧穿效应被认为能为量子计算提供加速，但除了一些学术概念验证（如HWS问题）外，其算法收益尚待深入探索。本文旨在将对HWS问题的分析扩展到更一般的势能函数，以理解隧穿效应在更广泛场景下的作用机制。

Method: 首先建立离散1维薛定谔算子的基态对数凹性框架，涵盖凸势能及某些局部极小值势能；然后利用该对数凹性结果，将Reichardt (2004)针对HWS的微扰分析扩展到具有对数凹性基态的更大家族势能，具体实例是从线性势推广到二次势。

Result: 建立了离散对数凹性作为基态的关键结构性质；证明了凸势能及某些局部极小值势能下基态的对数凹性；给出了超越Jarret和Jordan (2014)凸势能结果的新的谱隙界；将对数凹性结果用于扩展HWS微扰分析，成功从线性势推广到二次势。

Conclusion: 研究表明隧穿效应对凸势能加尖峰问题具有更广泛的适用性，为理解绝热量子优化中量子加速的机制提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: Quantum tunneling is expected to provide a computational speedup in quantum computing, a phenomenon that Adiabatic Quantum Optimization (AQO) aims to leverage. While some academic proofs of concept have been studied, such as the "Hamming weight with a spike" (HWS) problem, the algorithmic gains of this effect remain underexplored. In this work we extend the analysis underlying HWS to more general potentials.
  In the first half of the work, we establish (discrete) log-concavity of the ground state as a key structural property in this context. We devise a framework for establishing log-concavity of the ground state for a large family of discrete, 1-dimensional Schrödinger operators. The family includes convex potentials, but also certain potentials with local minima. In the convex case, this provides a discrete version of a continuous result by Brascamp and Lieb ('76). We demonstrate the utility of our result by establishing new spectral gap bounds, going beyond related results by Jarret and Jordan ('14) for convex potentials.
  In the second half of the work, we use our results on log-concavity to extend the perturbative analysis of HWS by Reichardt ('04) to the larger family of potentials with log-concave ground state. As a concrete instantiation, we use our result to extend the HWS analysis from a linear potential (which is exactly solvable) to a quadratic potential (which is no longer solvable). Our result strongly suggests the broader applicability of tunneling to convex potentials with spikes

</details>


### [123] [Genuine certification of incompatible quantum instruments through sequential communication tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23647)
*Arindam Mitra,Satyaki Manna,Debashis Saha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子仪器不相容性的操作意义，通过引入三类通信任务，证明了不相容的量子仪器可以实现经典通信无法达到的性能，从而实现了对不相容量子仪器的半设备无关认证，且该认证不依赖于仪器所诱导的测量和信道的相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子仪器是量子动力学的通用描述，其不相容性是量子理论中非经典性的基本表现。然而，这种不相容性的操作意义尚未被充分建立，需要一种能够实际认证不相容量子仪器的方法。

Method: 引入一类涉及发送方、中继方和接收方的三方通信任务，推导了所有相容仪器可实现性能的紧上界，该上界与经典通信在相同维度约束下的最优性能一致。违反该上界即可认证中继方实施的量子仪器对是不相容的。

Result: 该认证协议是真正的（genuine），能够认证一对仪器的不相容性，即使这些仪器各自诱导的测量和信道是两两相容的。同时识别了能够实现认证的最简通信场景实例。

Conclusion: 不相容仪器认证是通信中量子优势的一种表现形式，为量子仪器不相容性的操作意义提供了新见解，并建立了半设备无关认证框架。

Abstract: Quantum instruments constitute the general description of quantum dynamics, encompassing both quantum measurements and quantum channels as special cases. Consequently, the incompatibility of quantum instruments represents a fundamental manifestation of nonclassicality in quantum theory. Here, we establish the operational significance of this notion by demonstrating communication tasks with classical inputs and outputs that enable the semi-device-independent certification of incompatible quantum instruments. We introduce a class of three-party communication tasks involving a sender, a relayer, and a receiver, and derive the tight upper bound of the figure of merits of these tasks achievable by all compatible instruments implemented by the relayer and this bound coincides with the optimal performance attainable in a classical communication subject to the same dimensional constraints. Violation of this bound certifies the incompatibility of the pair of quantum instruments implemented by the relayer. This identifies certification of incompatible instruments as a manifestation of quantum advantage in communication. This certification protocol is genuine as it is able to certify the incompatibility of a pair of instruments where the measurements and channels induced by the instruments are pairwise compatible and, therefore it does not depend on the incompability of measurements and channels induced by the instruments. Finally, we identify the simplest instances of our communication scenario that enable the certification of incompatible quantum instruments.

</details>


### [124] [Robust Structure Learning of $k$-local Lindbladians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23652)
*Tim Möbus,Thiago Bergamaschi,Daniel Stilck França,Cambyse Rouzé*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效协议，用于从产品态制备、短时演化和单量子比特泡利测量中学习n量子比特上的未知k-局域林德布拉德生成元，无需先验知识。该协议在固定k和有界加权相互作用强度下，使用约Õ_k(ε^{-2}n^{2k}log(1/δ))个样本和多项式对数级演化时间，即可估计所有哈密顿量和耗散泡利-GKSL系数至精度ε。通过半定投影，可用约Õ_k(ε^{-2}n^{4k}log(1/δ))个样本和多项式时间经典后处理得到有效k-局域林德布拉德生成元。在满足稳定稀疏性条件下，n的依赖可从多项式降至对数级别。还提供了鲁棒结构学习过程、模型错误指定的保证，以及互补的样本复杂度下界。这是首个在如此有限实验控制下对一般k-局域耗散量子动力学的高效学习保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子动力学学习协议通常需要高度实验控制（如完全状态层析、长时演化或先验知识），但实际量子系统缺乏这些条件。作者旨在解决在有限实验资源下（仅产品态制备、短时演化、单比特泡利测量）高效学习一般k-局域耗散量子动力学的问题。

Method: 1) 利用短时演化展开，将林德布拉德生成元的参数估计转化为统计估计问题；2) 使用产品态制备和单量子比特泡利测量收集数据；3) 通过半定规划投影将估计值转换为有效的k-局域林德布拉德生成元；4) 针对稀疏结构提供改进算法；5) 提供鲁棒结构学习程序处理模型错误指定。

Result: 1) 一般情况：Õ_k(ε^{-2}n^{2k}log(1/δ))样本估计系数，Õ_k(ε^{-2}n^{4k}log(1/δ))样本获得钻石范数误差≤ε的有效林德布拉德生成元；2) 稀疏情况：仅需Õ_k(ε^{-2}log(n/δ))样本；3) 适用于长程相互作用衰减情况；4) 提供样本复杂度下界。

Conclusion: 这是首个在受限实验控制下（产品态制备、短时演化、单比特测量）对一般k-局域耗散量子动力学的可证明高效学习协议，并提供了完整的信息论上下界。该成果为量子动力学学习在实验可及条件下提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We present an efficient protocol for learning an unknown $k$-local Lindblad generator on $n$ qubits using only product-state preparations, short-time evolution, and single-qubit Pauli measurements, without prior knowledge of the interaction structure. For fixed $k$ and bounded weighted interaction strength, the protocol estimates all Hamiltonian and dissipative Pauli--GKSL coefficients to entrywise accuracy $\varepsilon$ with probability at least $1-δ$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{2k}\log(1/δ))$ samples and polylogarithmically many evolution times. A semidefinite projection converts these estimates into a valid $k$-local Lindblad generator with diamond-norm error at most $\varepsilon$ using $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}n^{4k}\log(1/δ))$ samples and polynomial-time classical postprocessing. If a suitable set of influential coefficients is supplied and satisfies a stable sparsity condition, the dependence on $n$ can improve from polynomial to logarithmic; in particular, exact supports of bounded intersection degree require only $\widetilde{\mathcal O}_k(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(n/δ))$ samples, with analogous reductions in system-size dependence for sufficiently decaying long-range interactions. We also provide a robust structure-learning procedure, extend the guarantees to model misspecification, and prove complementary sample-complexity lower bounds. To our knowledge, these are the first efficient learning guarantees for general $k$-local dissipative quantum dynamics under such limited experimental control.

</details>
